| So you decided to work with Arasaka. You landed on an operating table in some fancy corp clinic, and soon you'll be rid of the biochip.\n\nBut know this – you'll never get rid of me. Johnny Silverhand became part of the old V. He created a hybrid, the hybrid you still talk to out of old, tired habit, though you're really only talking to yourself. ## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

1524 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 340 distinct direct resource identities; 49 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

## Scene performance events

Section event data retains actor/animation/audio bindings and authored time fields, with raw pointers. Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.
